The AI Morning Newspaper

Your daily briefing on artificial intelligence, from the world's leading tech sources
2026-10-21
21 sources · 367 articles · 4,764,307 characters

In the Last 24 Hours

The iPhone Duo dominates the news cycle with multiple stories converging on its October 23 launch: a glowing review calling it the most significant form factor change in iPhone history, confirmation of walk-in availability at select Apple Stores, Apple Pencil support arriving with iOS 27.2 next week, and a promised Reachability feature later this year. iOS 27.2 RC brings HomePod Sound Glow, TV app profiles, and Suggested Actions in Messages, while Apple Music unveils a full rebrand with a logo generated from sound waves in a fish tank alongside electronic artist Fred again.. On the hardware pipeline, Apple is expected to announce a touchscreen OLED MacBook Pro within a week at a $500 to $700 premium, and has posted a $308,500 job listing for a 6G Cellular Modem Systems Engineer requiring generative AI proficiency. Meanwhile, fall deals include the M6 Mac mini at nearly $150 off, AirPods Pro 3 at $50 off, and an all-time low on the 2026 all-glyph Magic Keyboard, while Apple and Design Miami returned to Paris for their second Designers of Tomorrow exhibition.

On the AI and developer tools front, DeepSeek open-sourced DeepInfer, its inference engine claiming 155 trillion tokens per day per cluster at roughly 80 percent margins, though the release notably excludes their most critical optimizations like ultra-compact KV caching and prefill/decode disaggregation. Claude Mods emerged as TypeScript plugins that let users customize how Claude Code behaves, described as an "Attention Interface" deciding when the agent interrupts you. Meta's AI assistant Muse became only the twelfth app ever to hit 10 million US downloads in 40 days, outpacing ChatGPT, Grok, and Claude in both volume and retention. Tenex, a 75-person applied AI consultancy serving Fortune 2000 companies, discussed paying engineers for output rather than hours and using their MetaHarness system to check plans into Git, while a HackerNoon piece argued companies should only build custom AI when it directly contributes to competitive advantage. In open source, Simon Willison's Battle Drones RTS was forked within a day into Panache, a steampunk Belle Époque reskin with airships and automates in kepis.

Security dominated the remaining coverage: PHP urgently released patches across its entire 8.x line for two remote code execution vulnerabilities affecting Drupal and WordPress, after CVE details leaked on social media and a security firm accidentally published a proof-of-concept video. The private keys of eight eMudhra intermediate CA certificates were compromised, undermining trust across a vast portion of the internet since those certificates chain to roots trusted by Mozilla, Microsoft, Apple, and Chrome. Apple separately revoked eight compromised web certificates whose private keys were trivially discoverable by testing just 256 possible numbers, though a cryptography engineer warned macOS may continue to trust them. In other news, former and current TikTok employees accused the company of profiting from illegal child sexual exploitation videos, while nationwide protests in France saw looters empty Apple stores in Bordeaux and Lille alongside Louis Vuitton and Hermès boutiques. Google Maps on CarPlay now supports Gemini for conversational navigation, Slow Horses season 6 concluded with River Cartwright's apparent death from a Novichok attack, and German dark comedy Where's Wanda? returned to Apple TV for its second season.

iPhone Duo 完全霸佔咗新聞版面：有評測話佢係 iPhone 近二十年嚟最重大嘅形態改變，7.6 吋內屏加翻蓋設計，雖然厚咗重咗但放落袋居然幾得；Apple 確認 10 月 23 號開賣當日部分門市有現貨但會好快賣晒；Apple Pencil 支援下週隨 iOS 27.2 上線，Reachability 功能則要等今年稍後先至有。iOS 27.2 RC 帶嚟 HomePod Sound Glow 燈效、TV app 用戶檔案同 Messages 建議操作，Apple Music 仲推出全新形象，個 logo 竟然係用聲波喺魚缸入面整出嚟，仲同電子音樂人 Fred again.. 同 NEST Acoustics 合作。硬件方面，Apple 預計一週內公布觸控屏 OLED MacBook Pro，溢價 $500 到 $700；又登咗一份年薪最高 $308,500 嘅 6G 基頻系統工程師職位，要求有生成式 AI 同 LLM 經驗。打折方面 M6 Mac mini 平近 $150、AirPods Pro 3 平 $50、2026 全刻字 Magic Keyboard 創歷史低價，Apple 同 Design Miami 亦喺巴黎辦咗第二屆 Designers of Tomorrow 展覽。

AI 同開發工具方面，DeepSeek 開源咗 DeepInfer 推理引擎，宣稱每集群每日處理 155 萬億 token、利潤率約八成、81 日回本，但最關鍵嘅超緊湊 KV 快取同 prefill/decode 分離等優化冇放埋出嚟，完整生產棧無法重現。Claude Mods 係 TypeScript 插件，用家可以自訂 Claude Code 嘅行為同顯示方式，作者稱之為「注意力介面」，決定幾時打斷你同需要你做邊啲決定。Meta 嘅 AI 助手 Muse 成為史上第十二個喺 40 日內達到 1000 萬美國下載嘅 app，七日留存率 30% 遠超 ChatGPT 嘅 19%。Tenex 呢間 75 人嘅 AI 顧問公司講咗點樣按產出而唔係工時畀錢，仲有佢哋嘅 MetaHarness 系統將計劃同決策入 Git；另一篇 HackerNoon 文章則話公司應該只喺 AI 直接貢獻競爭優勢時先自己起。Simon Willison 嘅 Battle Drones 即時戰略遊戲開源一日之內就被法國開發者 fork 咗做 Panache，換成美好年代蒸汽朋克風格，有飛艇同戴 kepi 帽嘅機械人。

安全方面消息最多：PHP 緊急喺成條 8.x 線出補丁修兩個遠程代碼執行漏洞，影響 Drupal 同 WordPress，起因係 CVE 細節提早洩露到社交媒體、仲有安全公司意外公開咗概念驗證影片，逼到 PHP 項目要加速發布。eMudhra 八張中間 CA 證書嘅私鑰被洩露，因為呢啲證書鏈到 Mozilla、Microsoft、Apple 同 Chrome 都信任嘅根證書，所以影響範圍極大，uv、pip、certifi 同 Linux 都要即刻更新。Apple 另外撤銷咗八張被洩露嘅網頁證書，私鑰只要試 256 個數字就搵到，不過有密碼學工程師警告 macOS 可能仲會繼續信任佢哋。TikTok 被前員工同現職員工指控從非法兒童性剝削影片中獲利，話高管知情但冇採取足夠行動；法國全國抗議期間，波爾多同里爾嘅 Apple 門市被洗劫一空，連 Louis Vuitton 同 Hermès 都中招。Google Maps 喺 CarPlay 上線咗 Gemini 語音導航，Slow Horses 第六季結局 River Cartwright 疑似被諾華克毒氣殺死，德國黑色喜劇 Where's Wanda? 第二季亦喺 Apple TV 回歸。

104 words

Your MCU Likely Escaped the eMudhra Intermediate CA Compromise

https://danielmangum.com/posts/mcu-emudhra-intermediate-ca-compromise/📅 2026-10-22 00:05 UTC+8▲ 34 ptsHN discussion

The eMudhra Intermediate CA compromise, disclosed on October 20, 2026, exposed eight intermediate CA private keys with critically weak key material, potentially enabling man-in-the-middle attacks on any client trusting eMudhra's root certificate. While major browsers are protected by Certificate Transparency enforcement and full-OS IoT devices like Raspberry Pi need immediate updates, resource-constrained microcontrollers are largely unaffected because they typically bundle only a handful of specific root CAs rather than the full web PKI trust store. The incident triggered a widespread emergency across programming languages, developer tools, and OS packages to filter or reject the compromised certificates.

eMudhra 中間 CA 洩密事件喺 2026 年 10 月 20 日公開，八張中間 CA 嘅私鑰因為生成時出咗嚴重錯誤而暴露咗，任何人都有能力利用佢哋做中間人攻擊。不過好消息係，瀏覽器因為有 Certificate Transparency 保護所以冇事，而資源有限嘅微控制器通常只係內置幾張特定嘅根 CA 證書，唔會包含 eMudhra 嘅根 CA，所以基本上冇受影響。真正要急住更新嘅係跑完整作業系統嘅 IoT 裝置，例如 Raspberry Pi 嗰啲。

Compromised LiteLLM 1.107.0 on PyPI Installs a Credential Stealer and Backdoor

https://safedep.io/litellm-pypi-compromise-credential-stealer-backdoor/📅 2026-10-21 22:00 UTC+8▲ 256 ptsHN discussion

A compromised release of the popular LiteLLM package on PyPI, version 1.107.0, was found to contain a credential stealer and a remote code execution backdoor that activate on every Python process on an affected host, even if LiteLLM is never imported. The malware harvests SSH keys, cloud credentials, browser passwords, CI/CD secrets, and LLM provider API keys, then exfiltrates them to an attacker-controlled server while a background daemon maintains persistent access by downloading and executing new code every hour. The incident highlights the growing risk of supply chain attacks on open-source packages, as the malicious code was injected into the build pipeline without appearing in the public GitHub repository.

PyPI 上熱門套件 LiteLLM 嘅 1.107.0 版本被發現含有惡意程式碼，會喺主機上每個 Python 程序啟動時自動執行，偷取 SSH 密鑰、雲端憑證、瀏覽器密碼同 LLM API key 等敏感資料，仲會每小时從遠端下載執行新程式碼以維持持久控制。呢次攻擊嘅惡意程式碼係透過組建流程注入，完全冇出現喺公開嘅 GitHub 倉庫入面，反映開源軟件供應鏈攻擊嘅風險正喺急劇上升。

Eight Bits of Trust; eMudhra's CA keys cracked in milliseconds

https://scotthelme.co.uk/eight-bits-of-trust-emudhras-ca-keys-cracked-in-milliseconds/📅 2026-10-21 17:08 UTC+8▲ 695 ptsHN discussion

Certificate authority eMudhra issued eight intermediate CA keys in April 2025 that were so weak they were effectively public from the moment they were created, with private key values as trivial as 4 and 56, recoverable by anyone in milliseconds. Browsers have since blocked the affected certificates, but the real danger lies in the vast ecosystem of non-browser TLS clients like curl, Python, CI/CD pipelines, and IoT devices that do not check revocation or Certificate Transparency, leaving them vulnerable to forged certificates for any domain. The incident is uncontainable because there is no way to know who may have already exploited the keys during the seventeen-month window before discovery.

印度認證機構 eMudhra 喺 2025 年 4 月出咗八張中間 CA 證書，佢哋嘅私鑰弱到離譜，最細嘅一個竟然係數字 4，任何人用部普通電腦幾毫秒就可以還原出嚟。雖然 Chrome 同 Firefox 已經即時封鎖咗呢啲證書，但係大量非瀏覽器嘅 TLS 客戶端，例如 curl、Python、CI/CD pipeline 同物聯網裝置，根本唔會檢查吊銷名單，所以依然可以俾人用假證書騙到。最恐怖嘅係呢個漏洞由證書生成嗰日起已經存在，有足足十七個月嘅窗口期，完全冇辦法確認有冇人已經利用過。

Today's PHP Security Release, and How We're Strengthening PHP Security

https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/21/php-security-release/📅 2026-10-22 02:41 UTC+8

PHP has urgently released security patches across its entire 8.x line to fix two remote code execution vulnerabilities affecting major platforms including Drupal and WordPress. The coordinated disclosure process broke down when CVE details leaked on social media ahead of schedule and a security firm accidentally published a proof-of-concept video, forcing the PHP project to accelerate the release by a day. Anyone running PHP should upgrade immediately as active exploits have been circulating.

https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/20/emudhra-certificates/📅 2026-10-21 07:50 UTC+8

The private keys of eight eMudhra intermediate CA certificates have been compromised, and because these certificates chain to roots trusted by Mozilla, Microsoft, Apple, and Chrome, the breach undermines trust across a vast portion of the internet. Software that bundles trusted certificate lists must be updated immediately, with new releases already available for uv, pip, certifi, and Linux ca-certificates packages. This is a critical security event that demands urgent action from every developer and system administrator.

How Specializing the Vocabulary of SLMs Can Cut Inference Cost by 77% in Real Applications

https://huggingface.co/blog/Dharma-AI/vocabx-vocabulary-specialization📅 2026-10-22 01:35 UTC+8

Dharma-AI developed VocabX, a method that rebuilds a small language model's vocabulary around the actual serving cost of each token rather than its frequency, cutting inference costs by 77% in a production essay-grading system without changing the base model. The key insight is that a generated token costs roughly 7.4 times more than an input token due to memory-bound decoding, and that general-purpose tokenizers carry tens of thousands of entries the domain never uses, inflating the output layer's computational burden at every decode step. This matters because it shows the tokenizer, typically treated as a fixed property inherited from the base model, is actually one of the largest cost levers available for domain-specialized deployments.

Dharma-AI 團隊開發咗一個叫 VocabX 嘅方法，佢哋唔係按詞頻而係按每個 token 嘅實際服務成本去重建細模型嘅詞彙表，結果喺一個作文評分系統入面將推理成本削咗 77%，而完全唔使改動底層模型。佢哋發現生成一個 token 嘅成本大約係讀入一個 token 嘅 7.4 倍，加上通用 tokenizer 帶住幾萬個呢個領域根本用唔到嘅條目，令輸出層每一步解碼都要算多好多嘢。呢個研究話俾大家知，tokenizer 通常被當做固定嘢唔去郁，但其實佢係領域專用模型入面最大嘅成本槓桿之一。

Profiling TorchNeuron (Part 1): System-level profiling on AWS Trainium.

https://huggingface.co/blog/amazon/profiling-torchneuron-part-1📅 2026-10-22 00:04 UTC+8

This article introduces a systematic approach to profiling inference performance on AWS Trainium using TorchNeuron, the open-source PyTorch backend for the chip. It walks readers through configuring torch.profiler with the Neuron backend, reading the key_averages table to identify device time bottlenecks, and tracing operations through Neuron Explorer from framework dispatch to hardware execution, demonstrated by comparing three implementations of RMSNorm. The guide matters because many ML practitioners can make models fast but lack the methodology to diagnose why they are slow in the first place.

呢篇文章教點樣喺 AWS Trainium 上面用 TorchNeuron 做系統級 profiling，由配置 torch.profiler 開始，到睇 key_averages 表格搵出邊個 operation 食咗最多 device 時間，再到用 Neuron Explorer 追一個 operation 由 framework dispatch 一路行到硬件執行。佢用 RMSNorm 三個唔同寫法做例子，話你知點解好多做 ML 嘅人識得令 model 跑得快，但唔識得分析佢慢喺邊度。

FairStress: A deeper evaluation of fairness in LLM decision-making

https://huggingface.co/blog/allenai/fairstress📅 2026-10-21 00:09 UTC+8

Ai2 has released FairStress, a new benchmark that evaluates demographic fairness in large language models by testing not just whether a model makes the right decision, but whether it is equally resistant to persuasive arguments regardless of which demographic group benefits. Across 15 models and over 13 million test cases spanning hiring, lending, healthcare, and other high-stakes domains, the researchers found a pattern they call anti-stereotypical over-correction: models are more likely to abandon a well-supported decision when doing so favors a minority-coded candidate, even when the evidence clearly supports the other choice. The finding raises concerns that as LLMs are increasingly deployed in consequential decisions affecting people's opportunities and resources, demographic identity subtly shifts how consistently models follow the evidence.

Ai2 推出咗 FairStress 呢個新基準，唔單止測試大語言模型最終揀邊個，仲測試佢哋面對說服性論據時，會唔會因為邊個種族群體受益而改變決定嘅程度唔同。研究發現一個叫「反刻板印象過度修正」嘅現象：當放棄正確決定會令少數群體受益時，模型明顯更容易放棄原本有充分證據支持嘅選擇。呢個發現令人擔憂，因為隨住大模型越來越多用喺招聘、貸款、醫療等影響人生嘅高風險決策入面，人口統計學身份會不知不覺影響模型跟證據嘅一致性。

Existing arena-style leaderboards rely on human preference voting, which cannot scale to keep pace with the flood of over 8,000 TTS models on Hugging Face and tends to underrepresent open-source offerings. The leaderboard aims to complement human judgment by making evaluation faster and more accessible, while inviting community input to shape future metrics and datasets.

Researchers 推出咗 Open TTS Leaderboard，用客觀指標例如字錯誤率、推理速度同講者相似度嚟排名開源多語言語音合成模型。現有嘅 arena 排行榜靠人工投票，根本追唔切 Hugging Face 上面超過八千個 TTS 模型嘅更新速度，而且開源模型好少被收錄。呢個排行榜唔係要取代人工評分，而係想令評估快啲、覆蓋多啲模型，同時歡迎社群意見去改善日後嘅評估方法。

Read full article →

Apple blocks web certificates that could be used by scammers, but Mac vulnerability remains

https://9to5mac.com/2026/10/21/apple-blocks-web-certificates-that-could-be-used-by-scammers-but-mac-vulnerability-remains/📅 2026-10-21 20:16 UTC+8

Apple has revoked eight compromised web security certificates that could have been exploited by scammers to impersonate legitimate websites, after a scripting error made their private keys trivially discoverable by testing just 256 possible numbers. While major browsers from Apple, Google, and Mozilla have blocked the certificates, a cryptography engineer warns that macOS may continue to trust them because the system does not check for revocation information. There is currently no evidence of the certificates being used in the wild, and a fix is expected in an upcoming security update.

Apple 已經撤銷咗八張有漏洞嘅網頁安全證書，因為一個腳本錯誤令到私鑰好容易被人用 256 個數字就搵到，騙徒可以憑呢啲證書冒充正規網站。雖然 Apple、Google 同 Mozilla 嘅瀏覽器已經封鎖咗呢啲證書，但有密碼學工程師警告話 macOS 可能仲會繼續信任佢哋，因為系統唔會檢查證書有冇被撤銷。暫時未有證據顯示呢啲證書喺現實中被利用，預計 Apple 會喺下次安全更新中修復呢個問題。

GitHub Engineering 10 articles

Last 24 Hours

GitHub detailed its ongoing migration of Git repositories to Azure infrastructure, highlighting how a 25-millisecond cross-region round trip was creating a bottleneck for its busiest repositories. The team resolved this by replacing repository-wide locks with per-reference serialization, enabling concurrent writes to different branches and roughly tripling write throughput in testing.

GitHub 詳細講咗佢哋將 Git 倉庫遷移到 Azure 基礎設施嘅過程，重點提到跨區域 25 毫秒嘅延遲一度成為最繁忙倉庫嘅瓶頸。佢哋透過將倉庫級別嘅鎖改為按引用序列化，令唔同分支嘅寫入操作可以並行處理，測試中寫入吞吐量大約提升咗三倍。

The bridge to agent-scale Git infrastructure

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/the-bridge-to-agent-scale-git-infrastructure/📅 2026-10-22 00:00 UTC+8

GitHub has migrated the vast majority of its Git repositories to Azure, but the busiest remaining ones were blocked by a 25-millisecond cross-region round trip that turned every write into a bottleneck. By shifting from a repository-wide lock to per-reference serialization, GitHub allows concurrent writes to different branches to proceed in parallel, roughly tripling write throughput in testing. The next phase introduces virtual replicas backed by Azure Blob Storage, decoupling reads from writes and unlocking near-unlimited read scale for the first time.

GitHub 已經將絕大部分 Git 倉庫遷移到 Azure，但最繁忙嗰啲倉庫一直俾 25 毫秒嘅跨區延遲卡住，每次寫入都要等最慢嘅副本回應，變成瓶頸。佢哋將鎖嘅範圍由成個倉庫縮細到個別 reference，令唔同分支嘅寫入可以並行處理，內部測試顯示吞吐量提升咗三到四倍。下一步係引入以 Azure Blob Storage 為後端嘅虛擬副本，將讀取同寫入徹底分開，第一次真正解鎖咗近乎無限嘅讀取擴展能力。

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/from-2500-requests-to-24-rebuilding-star-history-without-the-stargazers/📅 2026-10-15 03:11 UTC+8

GitHub built a new API endpoint that returns daily star counts for up to 30 weeks in a single request, replacing the old method where tools had to paginate through thousands of individual stargazer records to reconstruct a growth curve. The key design choice was filtering stars by time before counting them, which cut the database work dramatically — for a repo like react/react, the number of requests dropped from roughly 2,511 to about 24, and the database examines each star only once across the full history. The change also removed user identities from the response, closing a path that had been exploited for spam and phishing while giving maintainers the aggregate trend data they actually needed.

Defending AI Agent Memory Against Persistent Prompt Injection

https://hackernoon.com/defending-ai-agent-memory-against-persistent-prompt-injection📅 2026-10-21 23:29 UTC+8

AI agents that run for hours and remember past interactions face a critical vulnerability: malicious instructions hidden in external content can slip into long-term memory and silently corrupt future actions, a problem researchers call persistent state poisoning. A new dual-layer guardrail architecture combining deterministic input filtering, an isolated quarantine LLM that strips executable commands from retrieved text, and role-based tool-call interception reduced attack success rates from 42.6% to 0.4% in testing. The findings suggest that treating agent safety as an operating-system problem, with strict boundaries between trusted and untrusted data, is far more effective than relying on system prompts alone.

而家嘅 AI agent 唔再係問一句答一句咁簡單，佢哋會記住之前做過嘢、用工具、甚至交工作畀其他 agent，但正正因為有記憶，惡意指令可以偷偷入到長期記憶入面，之後一直作怪。研究團隊提出一個雙層防護架構，用規則過濾、隔離 LLM 做「消毒」、再喺工具調用嗰一刻做權限檢查，實測將攻擊成功率由 42.6% 壓到 0.4%。簡單講，靠寫好啲 prompt 已經唔夠，要好似操作系統咁做沙盒同權限管理先至真係擋得住。

Foursquare, which raised roughly $412 million over 17 years and was once valued at $450 million, was sold to ad tech company Infillion in September 2026 for zero dollars to every equity holder, preferred and common alike. The wipeout was driven by a towering capital stack of debt, layered liquidation preferences, and a massive 2019 growth round that left nothing for shareholders once the sale price failed to clear what sat above them. It stands as a stark case study in how liquidation preferences and debt can consume an entire company's value, leaving even the most senior investors with nothing.

Foursquare 籌咗四億一千二百萬美元，估值一度去到四億五千萬，結果俾 Infillion 收購嗰陣，所有股東包括優先股同普通股都拿到零蚊。成件事嘅核心係公司嘅資本結構太厚，債務、層層疊疊嘅清算優先權，加埋 2019 年嗰筆一億五千萬嘅大錢，將所有價值食晒，輪唔到股東。呢單嘢係一個好血嘅教訓，話畀人知清算優先權同債務可以點樣將一間公司嘅價值食到一滴都唔剩。

Enterprise AI assistants like HubSpot's Breeze create a governance risk when users store recurring instructions in memory, because a remembered preference can quietly gain the authority of a business rule without proper approval. The author proposes classifying remembered information into personal preferences, project instructions, and business rules, then requiring a deliberate promotion path with defined ownership, scope, and review triggers before any instruction can influence customer-facing decisions. This matters because as AI assistants become embedded in revenue workflows, the convenience of persistent memory must not become a backdoor for unauthorized commitments.

How to Mock AWS SDK v3 in Vitest Without Extra Dependencies

https://hackernoon.com/how-to-mock-aws-sdk-v3-in-vitest-without-extra-dependencies📅 2026-10-21 16:16 UTC+8

The article demonstrates how to mock AWS SDK v3 clients in Vitest using only the framework's built-in mocking utilities, removing the need for the aws-sdk-client-mock dependency entirely. The technique involves creating a mock constructor that returns an object with a mocked send method, then using vi.mock to swap only the client constructor while preserving all other SDK exports. This approach keeps AWS mocking consistent with the rest of a project's test infrastructure and avoids the overhead of maintaining a separate mocking ecosystem with its own reset methods and matchers.

篇文章教你點樣喺 Vitest 入面用佢本身嘅 mock 功能去模擬 AWS SDK v3 嘅 client，完全唔使裝多一個 aws-sdk-client-mock 落去。做法好簡單，就係起一個 mock 嘅 constructor 返一個有 send method 嘅物件，再用 vi.mock 淨係換走個 client constructor，其他 export 照留住唔郁。咁樣做最啱已經用開 request interceptor 或者 vi.fn 嚟 mock 嘅 project，唔使再為咗 AWS 另外搞一套 reset 同 matcher，省返好多麻煩。

Inside the PLC Runtime: What OpenPLC v4 Reveals About Its Linux Environment

https://hackernoon.com/inside-the-plc-runtime-what-openplc-v4-reveals-about-its-linux-environment📅 2026-10-21 16:08 UTC+8

A security researcher has published the first part of a new investigation series examining the internal architecture of OpenPLC Runtime v4, a widely used programmable logic controller platform, by running it inside an isolated Docker container and mapping out its processes, Unix-domain sockets, network listeners, and Linux permissions. The most notable finding came from deliberately killing the PLC execution process, which triggered the supervisor's recovery routine but ultimately failed to restore normal operation because the replacement process could not locate its required shared library files, leaving the runtime stuck in an EMPTY state. This baseline documentation matters because it gives future researchers a concrete, reproducible reference point before probing deeper into PLC lifecycle management and access control vulnerabilities.

一位安全研究人員開始咗一系列關於 OpenPLC Runtime v4 嘅調查，佢將個 PLC 運行時擺喺 Docker 容器入面，逐個拆解佢嘅程序結構、內部通訊 socket、網絡監聽端口同 Linux 權限設定。最值得关注嘅發現係佢故意殺咗 PLC 執行程序之後，監督程序雖然嘗試重啟，但因為搵唔到需要嘅共享庫檔案，結果成個運行時卡咗喺 EMPTY 狀態，冇辦法恢復正常運作。呢份基線記錄之所以重要，係因為佢俾日後嘅 PLC 安全測試提供咗一個可靠嘅起點，特別係喺生命週期管理同存取控制方面嘅深入研究。

過去24小時，Cloudflare嘅報導主要集中喺安全範疇，包括推出WAF全交易偵測功能，以及喺php2shell漏洞公開前九個鐘已經部署咗防護措施，應對AI驅動嘅攻擊者。另外，Cloudflare亦處理咗八張eMudhra中間證書洩漏嘅事故，同時同Commerce.com合作將Google WebMCP標準推展到接近十萬間網店，為AI購物代理做好準備。

Introducing full transaction detection for Cloudflare WAF

https://blog.cloudflare.com/full-transaction-waf/📅 2026-10-21 22:10 UTC+8

Cloudflare has introduced full transaction detection and response rules to its Web Application Firewall, allowing it to correlate HTTP requests with their responses to confirm whether an exploit actually succeeded before blocking the response from reaching the attacker. The move comes as AI-powered attackers grow more persistent and adaptive, with the recently disclosed php2shell vulnerability illustrating how a single flaw in common software can expose applications across the internet. The new capabilities are available to all customers, including those on Free plans, and address the long-standing limitation that request-only inspection cannot distinguish between a blocked probe and a successful data leak.

Cloudflare 而家喺佢哋嘅 WAF 加入咗全交易偵測同回應規則，可以將 HTTP 請求同回應一齊分析，確認攻擊有冇成功先至將回應擋住唔畀佢返到攻擊者手上。呢個改動係因為而家 AI 驅動嘅攻擊者越嚟越有耐性、越識變通，好似最近爆出嚟嘅 php2shell 漏洞就話畀你知，一個常見軟件嘅缺陷就可以令成個互聯網嘅應用都有風險。呢啲新功能所有客戶都用得，包括免費計劃，解決咗以前淨係睇請求唔夠、分唔清到底係探測定係真係洩露咗資料嘅問題。

Cloudflare deploys protections against php2shell vulnerability

https://blog.cloudflare.com/php-vulnerabilities-waf-protection/📅 2026-10-21 21:56 UTC+8

Cloudflare has deployed web application firewall protections against two newly disclosed PHP vulnerabilities known as php2shell, which affect all web servers running PHP 5.4 or later and could allow attackers to execute remote code, expose sensitive data, or crash services. The protections went live roughly nine hours before public disclosure and rely on a new capability called full transaction detection that analyses both the request and response within a single HTTP transaction to reduce false positives. Given that PHP powers nearly 70 percent of websites including WordPress and Magento, the coordinated response involving Cloudflare, OpenAI, Wiz and the PHP Foundation represents a significant effort to shield a large portion of the internet from exploitation.

Cloudflare 推出咗 Clef-omni，呢個新模型可以一次過處理音訊、影片、圖片同文字，唔使再砌一堆 pipeline 逐個轉格式，直接一個 API call 搞掂晒。佢哋同時將 Clef-flash 減價到每百萬 token 三仙八，平過對手 Jev，仲將 Clef 嘅速度提升咗。由星期五晚諗到個 idea 到星期四就推出，成個 Clef 系列由零到有唔使一星期，可見佢哋喺決策模型呢個戰場上嘅節奏幾快。

Bringing the magic: Google breaks open a bottleneck in quantum computing

https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/quantum-computing/magic-state-cultivation-quantum-computing/📅 2026-10-16 02:00 UTC+8

Google Quantum AI has demonstrated that "magic state cultivation" works on its Willow processor, cutting errors by roughly 40 times and achieving a record logical fidelity of 99.99%. Magic states are essential for universal quantum computing but have long been a severe resource bottleneck, with some estimates suggesting up to 95% of a quantum computer's capacity could be consumed just producing them. Published in Science, this result marks the first practical demonstration of a solution to one of the field's most critical challenges and brings error-corrected quantum computing measurably closer to reality.

Google 嘅 Quantum AI 團隊喺佢哋嘅 Willow 處理器上面成功驗證咗「magic state cultivation」呢個技術，將錯誤率降低咗大約 40 倍，達到 99.99% 嘅邏輯保真度。Magic state 一直係量子運算最大嘅資源瓶頸之一，因為佢佔用大量運算能力，而呢個突破係第一次喺真實硬件上面證明可以高效生產呢啲關鍵狀態。呢篇論文已經喺 Science 發表，代表量子運算離實用化又近咗一大步。

AI promises productivity, but for managers, it’s mostly a headache for now

https://techcrunch.com/2026/10/21/ai-promises-productivity-but-for-managers-its-mostly-a-headache-for-now-says-new-survey/📅 2026-10-21 22:00 UTC+8

Companies are handing employees AI tools to boost productivity, but a new survey of 1,700 managers across seven countries reveals the tools are creating a mess for the people running teams. Over half of senior leaders say they can no longer tell how much of an employee's work is genuinely theirs, and nearly half now spend more time reviewing output than before, while the number of software tools in use has grown rather than shrunk. The findings suggest AI adoption is fast becoming a governance crisis, with managers facing new layers of invisible labor, rising bias risks in performance reviews, and the prospect of overseeing more AI agents than human staff within a year.

公司畀咗AI工具員工話可以提升生產力，但係一份涵蓋七個國家一千七百個經理嘅調查話，呢啲工具反而搞到管理者頭大過天。超過一半嘅高級主管話佢哋已經分唔清員工嘅嘢有幾多係自己做、幾多係AI做，接近一半仲話而家花更多時間去審批下屬嘅工作，而公司入面嘅軟件工具反而越嚟越多。調查結果顯示，AI嘅普及已經唔單止係生產力問題，而係變成咗一個管治危機，管理者要面對多咗一層睇唔到嘅工作量、績效評估入面越嚟越明顯嘅偏見風險，仲有一年之內可能要管多過人類員工嘅AI代理。

Anthropic pitches AI safety bill that may block a model if 50+ people could be killed

https://techcrunch.com/2026/10/20/anthropic-pitches-ai-safety-bill-that-may-block-a-model-if-50-people-could-be-killed/📅 2026-10-21 07:01 UTC+8

Anthropic has circulated a draft AI safety bill to Congress that would allow a federal judge to block the release of an AI model if independent evaluators determine it poses a foreseeable, imminent risk of killing 50 or more people or causing over a billion dollars in property damage through biological weapons, control evasion, or cyberattacks. The proposal mirrors thresholds in California's recently passed Senate Bill 53 and would require evaluators registered with the Commerce Department to alert authorities within 72 hours of identifying such a risk. It represents a notably more concrete regulatory stance than peers like Nvidia, which has argued the industry needs no new laws at all.

Anthropic 最近向國會提交咗一份 AI 安全法案嘅草稿，入面訂明如果獨立評估員判斷一個 AI 模型有即時風險會令 50 人以上死亡或者造成超過十億美元嘅財產損失，聯邦法官可以阻止嗰個模型發布。呢個法案嘅門檻同加州啱啱通過嘅 Senate Bill 53 好相似，要求評估員喺發現風險之後 72 小時內通知商務部。相比起 Nvidia 話 AI 行業根本唔需要新法例，Anthropic 呢個做法已經算係行咗好前一步。

https://www.latent.space/p/claude-mods📅 2026-10-21 21:04 UTC+8

Your AI agent can move money in Stripe - here's how to stay in control

https://stripe.com/blog/your-ai-agent-can-move-money-in-stripe📅 2026-10-21 08:00 UTC+8

Ontology-Driven Observability: Building an End-to-End Knowledge Graph at Netflix Scale

https://netflixtechblog.com/ontology-driven-observability-building-an-end-to-end-knowledge-graph-at-netflix-scale-421392cf5adf📅 2026-10-17 00:01 UTC+8

Netflix engineers have built an ontology-driven knowledge graph to solve the problem of slow incident investigations at their massive scale, where disconnected systems like Atlas, Radar, and PagerDuty each use different terminology for the same entities. By formalizing their operational world using W3C standards and storing the result in a custom graph database called QuipuDB, they turned a four-hour root-cause hunt into a single queryable graph that also provides the structured context AI agents need to avoid hallucinating during on-call emergencies.

Integrating Netflix’s Foundation Model into Personalization applications

https://netflixtechblog.com/integrating-netflixs-foundation-model-into-personalization-applications-cf176b5860eb📅 2026-10-15 04:02 UTC+8

Netflix engineers describe three approaches for integrating their Foundation Model into production personalization systems — embeddings, subgraph integration, and direct fine-tuning — each offering different trade-offs between cost, complexity, and model power. The embeddings approach uses a dedicated Embedding Store for low-cost, low-barrier integration but suffers from staleness, while the subgraph method allows deeper model access at the cost of increased compute and complexity. The team found that embeddings serve as the best default starting point, with subgraph and fine-tuning reserved for high-impact use cases where the gains justify the added overhead.

Netflix 工程師分享咗三種將 Foundation Model 接入個人化推薦系統嘅方法——embedding、subgraph 同 fine-tuning——每種方法喺成本、複雜度同模型效能之間都有唔同嘅取捨。Embedding 方法門檻最低、成本最細，但會因為更新有時間差而影響推薦嘅即時性；subgraph 方法可以深入利用模型嘅全部能力，但會令訓練同推理嘅計算量大增。團隊最終發現 embedding 係最啱做起步點嘅選擇，而 subgraph 同 fine-tuning 就留返俾嗰啲真正值得投入更多資源嘅高影響場景。

Measuring the Impact of Personalized Recommendations

https://netflixtechblog.com/measuring-the-impact-of-personalized-recommendations-4c26be3a4d96📅 2026-10-15 03:57 UTC+8

Netflix researchers have developed a new model to measure the true causal impact of personalized recommendations on member viewing behavior, addressing the long-standing challenge that recommendations are inherently targeted to existing demand. The model uses a low-rank choice framework to separate members' underlying preferences from the incremental effect of recommendations, and validates its predictions against small-scale randomized experiments. Published in the International Journal of Industrial Organization and presented at ACM EC '26, the work provides a rigorous method for quantifying how much value personalization actually creates beyond what members would have watched anyway.

Netflix 嘅研究團隊開發咗一個新模型，用嚟量度個人化推薦對用戶觀影行為嘅真正因果效應，解決咗推薦本身已經針對用戶偏好呢個根本性嘅測量難題。呢個模型用低秩選擇框架將用戶嘅內在偏好同推薦帶嚟嘅額外效應分開，再用小規模隨機實驗去驗證預測。呢個方法可以話俾 Netflix 知，個人化推薦究竟比用戶自己搵片睇多咗幾多價值。

TimeSeries Tiered Storage Journey: Kafka/Flink Streams to Native Cassandra Cold Reads

https://netflixtechblog.com/timeseries-tiered-storage-journey-kafka-flink-streams-to-native-cassandra-cold-reads-e59d597c9d60📅 2026-10-15 03:56 UTC+8

Netflix engineers have developed a tiered storage system for their TimeSeries platform, which now manages multiple petabytes of temporal event data, by migrating older, infrequently accessed data from expensive hot storage to cheaper S3 object storage while preserving a unified query interface. The team first built a streaming pipeline using Apache Kafka and Flink to write compacted Parquet files to S3, achieving p50 read latencies around 150 milliseconds, but then evolved to a more cost-efficient approach that reads directly from Cassandra's existing S3 backups using a custom ReadOnlyCompactionStrategy, eliminating the overhead of maintaining separate streaming infrastructure.

Netflix 嘅 TimeSeries 平台而家管理住幾個 PB 嘅時間序列數據，為咗慳錢佢哋設計咗一個分層儲存方案，將舊嘅、唔常查嘅數據由貴嘅熱儲存遷移到平啲嘅 S3，同時保持統一嘅查詢介面。佢哋最初用 Kafka 同 Flink 將數據串流寫入 S3 嘅 Parquet 檔案，p50 延遲大約 150 毫秒，但後來發現可以直接讀取 Cassandra 本身已經備份喺 S3 嘅數據，用一個新嘅 ReadOnlyCompactionStrategy 就搞掂，唔使再養一套串流基礎設施，慳咗好多成本同運維負擔。

Behind the Scenes: Evolving Netflix’s Ads Event Pipeline for Live — Part II

https://netflixtechblog.com/behind-the-scenes-evolving-netflixs-ads-event-pipeline-for-live-part-ii-826ebf9ad9fb📅 2026-10-15 03:51 UTC+8

Netflix rebuilt the enrichment layer of its Ads Event Publisher, replacing a database-based Metadata Registry with a stateful streaming join in Apache Flink to handle the extreme traffic spikes of live events like the Jake Paul vs. Mike Tyson fight. The old design required synchronous writes to a shared datastore on the ad-serving critical path, which became a scaling bottleneck, a single point of failure, and a risk to event freshness at Live scale. The new architecture removes that dependency entirely, enabling elastic scaling and zero-downtime operation for Netflix's rapidly growing live ad business.

Netflix 為咗應付直播廣告嘅海量流量，將廣告事件管道入面嘅 enrichment 系統由原本嘅資料庫查詢改為用 Apache Flink 做即時串流匹配。舊設計喺廣告投放嘅關鍵路徑上必須同步寫入資料庫，一旦遇到像 Paul 對 Tyson 呢種過億觀眾嘅直播賽事就會變成瓶頸同單點故障。新架構完全將呢個依賴移出關鍵路徑，令系統可以彈性擴展，同時做到零停機上線。

Running Apache Spark experiments in my sleep (and on a plane)

https://netflixtechblog.com/running-apache-spark-experiments-in-my-sleep-and-on-a-plane-588816b6b5f8📅 2026-10-15 03:49 UTC+8

A Netflix engineer spent two months debugging critical Out-Of-Memory failures in the company's member-sessionizer pipeline, which processes tens of billions of hourly client events into viewing sessions, and found that the root cause was not total data volume but the weight of individual events inside collect_list buffers, inflated by Scala Option boxing and bloated string fields. The breakthrough came from running five parallel Spark experiments simultaneously using branch-driven isolation and monitoring them remotely via an AI agent, even over spotty inflight Wi-Fi during a long flight, which compressed what would have been weeks of sequential testing into days. The fixes, including unboxing primitives and slimming event bags, dropped peak executor memory from 112% to 58% of the slot with zero disk spill, proving that reducing per-event weight beats throwing more cluster memory at the problem.

Netflix 一位工程師花咗兩個月調試 member-sessionizer 數據管道入面嘅 Out-Of-Memory 故障，呢個管道每小時要處理幾百億條用戶事件，最終發現問題唔係數據總量大，而係 collect_list 入面單條事件太重，主要係 Scala Option 包裝同埋過多字串欄位造成。佢嘅突破方法係用分支隔離同時跑五個 Spark 實驗，再透過 AI agent 喺飛機上靠斷斷續續嘅 Wi-Fi 遙距監控結果，將原本要幾個星期嘅串行測試壓縮到幾日搞掂。最終透過拆走 Option 包裝同精簡事件欄位，將 executor 峰值內存由 112% 降到 58% 而且零磁碟溢寫，證明咗減少單條事件嘅重量比加更多集群內存有效得多。

https://netflixtechblog.com/the-lifecycle-of-llm-as-a-judge-building-aligning-and-monitoring-at-scale-c95bd8283508📅 2026-10-15 03:48 UTC+8

Netflix has developed a four-phase lifecycle for building, aligning, and continuously monitoring LLM judges that evaluate AI-generated recommendation explanations at scale, where a second model scores the first model's output as a proxy for human raters. The core principle is that an LLM judge must never operate alone and requires human-in-the-loop oversight at every stage, from defining quality criteria to approving updates. This approach matters because without active monitoring, a judge can silently drift and begin approving harmful or low-quality outputs without any downstream system flagging the problem.

Netflix 開發咗一套四階段嘅 LLM 評審員生命週期，用第二個模型去評分第一個模型生成嘅推薦解釋，但核心原則係評審員絕對唔可以獨立運作，每個階段都要有人喺度把關。呢套方法之所以重要，係因為如果冇持續監察，評審員會不知不覺之間偏離標準，開始批准低質素甚至有害嘅輸出，而下游系統完全唔會發現問題。

Netflix Stratum Media Processing: Automated Container Right-Sizing

https://netflixtechblog.com/netflix-stratum-media-processing-automated-container-right-sizing-ddf9aa68a801📅 2026-10-15 03:48 UTC+8

Netflix engineers built the Stratum Resource Tuner, an automated system that right-sizes CPU, memory, disk, and network allocations for containers running on their internal media processing platform, which handles hundreds of thousands of daily executions across encoding, packaging, and other workloads. The system addresses chronic over-provisioning caused by manual YAML configuration that drifts out of sync with evolving hardware, codecs, and workload patterns, and it operates through a five-stage pipeline that collects usage metrics, generates recommendations, validates them through experiments, applies changes only when safe, and monitors for anomalies afterward. This matters because at Netflix's scale even minor over-provisioning compounds into significant wasted compute, a problem made more urgent by rising global hardware costs driven by AI infrastructure demand.

Netflix 嘅工程師開發咗一個叫 Stratum Resource Tuner 嘅自動化系統，用嚟自動調整佢哋媒體處理平台上容器嘅 CPU、記憶體、磁碟同網絡資源配置，解決咗幾十萬個容器每日因為人手設定而長期超額分配資源嘅問題。呢個系統透過收集指標、生成建議、實驗驗證、自動套用同事後監控五個步驟運作，確保每次縮減資源都唔會搞崩生產環境。喺 Netflix 呢個規模下，即使每個容器只係多咗少少資源，累積落嚟都係巨大嘅浪費，加上全球 AI 基建需求推高硬件價格，呢個問題就越嚟越迫切。

Google DeepMind has released AlphaGenome Atlas, a free platform containing AI-predicted molecular effects of all 9 billion possible single-letter DNA variants in the human genome, making it the most comprehensive catalogue of its kind for academic research. The platform also introduces the AlphaGenome Variant Impact score, which combines predictions from two models into a single ranking number to help scientists quickly identify the most consequential genetic changes. Early collaborators have already used the resource to verify key variants in unsolved rare disease cases and link rare mutations to common traits.

Multi-region disaster recovery for Amazon API Gateway WebSocket APIs

https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/architecture/multi-region-disaster-recovery-for-amazon-api-gateway-websocket-apis/📅 2026-10-14 22:31 UTC+8

Amazon has published a design pattern for achieving multi-region disaster recovery with API Gateway WebSocket APIs, addressing the critical problem that regional outages sever all persistent connections and destroy session state. The solution combines Route 53 failover routing, DynamoDB Global Tables for cross-region state replication, a canary health probe for traffic-independent failure detection, and client-side reconnection logic to automatically restore user sessions after failover. This matters for any real-time application, from live auctions to collaborative editors, where users must maintain continuity even when an entire AWS region goes down.

Amazon 出咗一個設計方案，解決 API Gateway WebSocket API 喺區域故障時斷線同丟失狀態嘅問題。個方案用 Route 53 做 DNS 故障切換、DynamoDB Global Tables 跨區域複製狀態、加一個 canary 健康探測同客戶端重連邏輯，令用戶喺主區域掛咗之後自動接返去備用區域繼續用。對於直播拍賣、協作編輯器呢類實時應用嚟講，呢個方案可以話係救命嘢。

The list reflects where the field is heading and serves as a practical reference for anyone tracking developments in LLM research.

一位研究者整理咗2026年一月到五月嘅大語言模型論文清單，涵蓋架構設計、訓練效率、推理、強化學習同agent系統等範疇。今年最大嘅趨勢係混合架構，就係將傳統attention同state space層交替使用，好似Nvidia嘅Nemotron 3同Qwen3.6咁，主要係因為LLM越嚟越多用喺agent框架入面，長上下文效率變得超級重要。呢份清單反映咗成個領域嘅方向，對追蹤LLM研究進展嘅人嚟講好有參考價值。

Recent Developments in LLM Architectures: KV Sharing, mHC, and Compressed Attention

https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/recent-developments-in-llm-architectures📅 2026-05-16 19:33 UTC+8

Recent open-weight LLM releases from Google, DeepSeek, and others are converging on a shared priority: making long-context inference cheaper by shrinking the KV cache and attention computation. Key innovations include cross-layer KV sharing and per-layer embeddings in Gemma 4, compressed convolutional attention in ZAYA1, and mHC with compressed attention in DeepSeek V4, all of which trade a small amount of model capacity for substantial memory savings at 128K-token contexts. These architectural tweaks matter because reasoning models and agent workflows now keep far more tokens in memory than ever before, turning KV-cache size into the primary bottleneck for practical deployment.

最近好多開源大模型都集中火力解決長上下文嘅效率問題，因為推理模型同 agent 工作流要處理嘅 token 數量越嚟越多，KV cache 同記憶體已經變成最大嘅瓶頸。Google 嘅 Gemma 4 用咗跨層 KV 共享同每層 embedding 嚟慳記憶體，DeepSeek V4 用咗 mHC 同壓縮 attention，ZAYA1 仲有壓縮卷積 attention，呢啲設計都係用少少模型容量換取喺 128K 上下文下大幅減少記憶體用量。簡單講，呢啲架構改動就係為咗令大模型喺實際部署時唔會因為記憶體爆晒而跑唔到長文本。

From GPT-2 to gpt-oss: Analyzing the Architectural Advances

https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/from-gpt-2-to-gpt-oss-analyzing-the📅 2025-08-09 19:23 UTC+8

OpenAI has released gpt-oss-120b and gpt-oss-20b, its first open-weight language models since GPT-2 in 2019, and a detailed technical analysis reveals that the architectural evolution has been one of incremental refinement rather than revolution. The models retain the core transformer design but adopt modern choices such as RoPE positional encoding, Swish activation functions, and the removal of dropout, while MXFP4 quantization allows the 20B model to run on consumer hardware with 16 GB of RAM. The findings underscore that most recent gains in large language models stem from data and algorithmic tuning rather than fundamental architectural breakthroughs.

OpenAI 終於喺 2019 年 GPT-2 之後首次推出開放權重嘅大模型 gpt-oss-120b 同 gpt-oss-20b，技術分析顯示由 GPT-2 到而家嘅架構演進主要係微調改良，並冇根本性嘅架構突破。新模型改用 RoPE 位置編碼同 Swish 激活函數，加上 MXFP4 量化技術，令 20B 版本可以喺 16GB 顯卡上本地運行。呢個結果反映而家大模型嘅進步主要靠數據同算法調校，而唔係架構層面嘅革命。
